CC   -!- FUNCTION: Guanine nucleotide-binding proteins (G proteins) are involved
CC       as a modulator or transducer in various transmembrane signaling systems
CC       (PubMed:29925951, PubMed:33762731, PubMed:34239069, PubMed:35610220,
CC       PubMed:35714614, PubMed:35835867, PubMed:36087581, PubMed:36989299,
CC       PubMed:37327704, PubMed:37935376, PubMed:37935377, PubMed:37963465,
CC       PubMed:37991948, PubMed:38168118, PubMed:38552625, PubMed:39103320).
CC       The beta and gamma chains are required for the GTPase activity, for
CC       replacement of GDP by GTP, and for G protein-effector interaction
CC       (PubMed:29925951, PubMed:33762731, PubMed:34239069, PubMed:35610220,
CC       PubMed:35714614, PubMed:35835867, PubMed:36087581, PubMed:36989299,
CC       PubMed:37327704, PubMed:37935376, PubMed:37935377, PubMed:37963465,
CC       PubMed:38168118, PubMed:38552625, PubMed:39103320). Involved in bitter
CC       and sweet taste transduction as well as in umami (monosodium glutamate,
CC       monopotassium glutamate, and inosine monophosphate) taste transduction
CC       (PubMed:40339580, PubMed:38600377, PubMed:38776963).
CC       {ECO:0000269|PubMed:29925951, ECO:0000269|PubMed:33762731,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:34239069, ECO:0000269|PubMed:35610220,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:35714614, ECO:0000269|PubMed:35835867,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:36087581, ECO:0000269|PubMed:36989299,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:37327704, ECO:0000269|PubMed:37935376,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:37935377, ECO:0000269|PubMed:37963465,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:37991948, ECO:0000269|PubMed:38168118,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:38552625, ECO:0000269|PubMed:38600377,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:38776963, ECO:0000269|PubMed:39103320,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:40339580}.
CC   -!- SUBUNIT: G proteins are composed of 3 units, alpha, beta and gamma
CC       (PubMed:29925951, PubMed:33762731, PubMed:34239069, PubMed:35610220,
CC       PubMed:35714614, PubMed:35835867, PubMed:36087581, PubMed:36989299,
CC       PubMed:37327704, PubMed:37935376, PubMed:37935377, PubMed:37963465,
CC       PubMed:38168118, PubMed:38552625, PubMed:39103320). The heterodimer
CC       formed by GNB1 and GNG2 interacts with ARHGEF5 (PubMed:19713215). The
CC       heterodimer formed by GNB1 and GNG2 interacts with GRK2 (By
CC       similarity). The heterodimer formed by GNB1 and GNG2 interacts with the
CC       ghrelin receptor GHSR (PubMed:35027551, PubMed:39833471). Forms a
CC       complex with GNAO1 and GNG3 (PubMed:34685729). Forms a complex with
CC       GNAO1 and GNG2; this complex associates with Mu-type opioid receptor
CC       OPRM1, D(2) dopamine receptor DRD2, neurotensin receptor type 1 NTSR1
CC       and beta-2 adrenergic receptor ADRB2 (PubMed:39103320). Interacts with
CC       ARHGEF18 and RASD2 (PubMed:14512443, PubMed:19255495). Forms complexes
CC       with TAS2R14 and G proteins; these complexes play a role in the
CC       perception of bitterness (PubMed:38600377, PubMed:38776963). Component
CC       of the TAS2R14-GNAI1 complex, consisting of TAS2R14, GNAI1, GNB1 and
CC       GNG2 (PubMed:38600377, PubMed:38776963). Component of the TAS2R14-GNAT3
CC       complex, consisting of TAS2R14, GNAT3, GNB1 and GNG2 (PubMed:38600377,
CC       PubMed:38776963). Component of the TAS2R14-GNAS2 complex, consisting of
CC       TAS2R14, GNAS2, GNB1 and GNG2 (PubMed:38776963). Component of the sweet
CC       taste receptor complex, consisting of TAS1R2, TAS1R3, GNAT3, GNB1 and
CC       GNG2; this complex mediates sweet taste transduction (PubMed:40339580).
CC       Interacts with MC1R, the interaction is important for coupling of MC1R
CC       to G protein (PubMed:34453129). {ECO:0000250|UniProtKB:P62871,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:14512443, ECO:0000269|PubMed:19255495,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:19713215, ECO:0000269|PubMed:29925951,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:33762731, ECO:0000269|PubMed:34239069,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:34453129, ECO:0000269|PubMed:34685729,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:35027551, ECO:0000269|PubMed:35610220,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:35714614, ECO:0000269|PubMed:35835867,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:36087581, ECO:0000269|PubMed:36989299,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:37327704, ECO:0000269|PubMed:37935376,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:37935377, ECO:0000269|PubMed:37963465,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:38168118, ECO:0000269|PubMed:38552625,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:38600377, ECO:0000269|PubMed:38776963,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:39103320, ECO:0000269|PubMed:39833471,
CC       ECO:0000269|PubMed:40339580}.

CC   -!- DISEASE: Intellectual developmental disorder, autosomal dominant 42
CC       (MRD42) [MIM:616973]: A disorder characterized by significantly below

CC       adaptive behavior and manifested during the developmental period. MRD42

CC       and poor growth. {ECO:0000269|PubMed:25485910,
